Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any biodiversity module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 35. The projective resolution has length at most 587 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on biodiversity structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 35. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 587. QED.
